# Khwarazm under the Afrigids, lifted from CK3's own flavorization: the
# duchy seat keeps Vilayah/Khwarezmshah under the county, the
# Khwarezmshahdom proper from there, Shahbanu consorted as presiding
# queen; a raise to kingdom reads Grand Amirate, to empire Malik
# al-Muazzam (islam_religion-gated rows won naturally).
# Viennois styles itself a Dauphinate under a Dauphin (CK3's d_dauphine,
# held with Lotharingia by Lothaire himself - the Provence pattern). Placed
# with the other specific blocks, ahead of default.
dauphine_dauphinate = {
	rank = {
		1 = COUNTY
		2 = DAUPHINATE
		3 = KINGDOM
		4 = EMPIRE
		5 = HEGEMONY
	}

	ruler_male = {
		1 = COUNT
		2 = DAUPHIN
		3 = KING
		4 = EMPEROR
		5 = EMPEROR
	}

	ruler_female = {
		1 = COUNTESS
		2 = DAUPHINE
		3 = QUEEN
		4 = EMPRESS
		5 = EMPRESS
	}

	consort_male = {
		1 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		2 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		3 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		4 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		5 = PRINCE_CONSORT
	}

	consort_female = {
		1 = CONSORT
		2 = DAUPHINE
		3 = QUEEN_CONSORT
		4 = EMPRESS_CONSORT
		5 = EMPRESS_CONSORT
	}

	heir_male = {
		1 = HEIR
		2 = HEIR
		3 = HEIR
		4 = EMPEROR
		5 = EMPEROR
	}

	heir_female = {
		1 = HEIR_fem
		2 = HEIR_fem
		3 = HEIR_fem
		4 = EMPRESS
		5 = EMPRESS
	}

	trigger = {
		government = monarchy
		OR = {
		tag = DAU
		}
	}
}

# The Finnic and Baltic worlds have no culture-specific ranks in CK3, so
# they use its default tribal track: chieftains below, high chieftains
# above. CK3's discriminator for the tribal track is the holder's government
# (only unreformed pagans are tribal in 867), which our realms cannot carry
# (all sit on feudalism_reform), so the block keys on the ruler's faith
# instead - the same split, provable per tag. Balaton joins by tag: Kocel's
# roman-rite Slavic principality is still a tribal realm in CK3's 867
# (user-confirmed 18 Oct 2026), the faith proxy under-fires for it. Pagan
# steppe (tengri) realms had no seat in the old culture-group net and ride
# this ladder now, like CK3. Norse tags are caught by the block above first.
tribal_chiefdoms = {
	rank = {
		1 = CHIEFDOM
		2 = HIGH_CHIEFDOM
		3 = KINGDOM
		4 = HIGH_KINGDOM
		5 = HEGEMONY
	}

	ruler_male = {
		1 = CHIEFTAIN
		2 = HIGH_CHIEFTAIN
		3 = KING
		4 = HIGH_KING
		5 = EMPEROR
	}

	ruler_female = {
		1 = CHIEFTESS
		2 = HIGH_CHIEFESS
		3 = QUEEN
		4 = HIGH_QUEEN
		5 = EMPRESS
	}

	consort_male = {
		1 = CHIEFTAIN
		2 = HIGH_CHIEFTAIN
		3 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		4 = PRINCE_CONSORT
		5 = PRINCE_CONSORT
	}

	consort_female = {
		1 = CHIEFTESS
		2 = HIGH_CHIEFESS
		3 = QUEEN_CONSORT
		4 = QUEEN_CONSORT
		5 = QUEEN_CONSORT
	}

	heir_male = {
		1 = HEIR
		2 = HEIR
		3 = HEIR
		4 = EMPEROR
		5 = EMPEROR
	}

	heir_female = {
		1 = HEIR_fem
		2 = HEIR_fem
		3 = HEIR_fem
		4 = EMPRESS
		5 = EMPRESS
	}

	trigger = {
		government = monarchy
		OR = {
			religion = slavic
			religion = romuva
			religion = suomenusko
			religion = tengri_pagan_reformed
			religion = norse_pagan_reformed
			religion = taltosist
			tag = BLT
		}
	}
}

		government = theocracy
	}
}

# Vanilla styles small HRE monarchies (dev < 50) as counties regardless of
# rank. With real tiers, only rank-1 realms are counties, decided above, so
# this block is killed with a trigger that can never fire.
hre_county = {
	rank = {
		1 = COUNTY
		2 = DUCHY
		3 = KINGDOM
		4 = EMPIRE
		5 = HEGEMONY

		3 = QUEEN
